local Kuri = game.Players.LocalPlayer

if getgenv().team then
    _G.SelectedTeam = getgenv().team
else
    _G.SelectedTeam = "Marines" 
end

repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded() and Kuri:FindFirstChild("DataLoaded")

if Kuri.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main (minimal)") then
    repeat
        task.wait()
        local l_Remotes_0 = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
        l_Remotes_0.CommF_:InvokeServer("SetTeam", _G.SelectedTeam)
        task.wait(3)
    until not Kuri.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main (minimal)")
end

local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "ToggleBtn"
gui.Parent = game.CoreGui
gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

local btn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
btn.Name = "ToggleButton"
btn.Parent = gui
btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)
btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0.1, 0)
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.Image = "rbxassetid://127632820302449" 
btn.Draggable = true

local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
corner.Parent = btn

btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    -- Gửi cả sự kiện nhấn xuống VÀ nhả ra
    local virtualInput = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
    
    -- Nhấn xuống
    virtualInput:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
    
    -- Nhả ra (rất quan trọng!)
    virtualInput:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
end)
  
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer

-- Tất cả placeId hợp lệ (cũ + mới)
local ValidPlaceIds = {
    2753915549,      -- Sea1 cũ
    4442272183,      -- Sea2 cũ
    7449423635,      -- Sea3 cũ
    85211729168715,  -- Sea1 mới
    79091703265657,  -- Sea2 mới
    100117331123089  -- Sea3 mới
}

-- Xác định Sea (hỗ trợ cả placeId cũ và mới)
local Sea1 = game.PlaceId == 85211729168715 or game.PlaceId == 2753915549
local Sea2 = game.PlaceId == 79091703265657 or game.PlaceId == 4442272183  
local Sea3 = game.PlaceId == 100117331123089 or game.PlaceId == 7449423635

-- Kiểm tra placeId hợp lệ
local IsValidPlace = false
for _, id in ipairs(ValidPlaceIds) do
    if game.PlaceId == id then
        IsValidPlace = true
        break
    end
end

if not IsValidPlace then
    plr:Kick("Unsupport game")
    return
end

function BKP(Point)
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = Point
    task.wait()
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = Point
end

local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
local isTeleporting = false
_G.StopTween = false
_G.TweenSpeed = 350 -- Mặc định

-- Biến cho noclip
local noclipConnection = nil
local originalCollisions = {}

-- CHECK SEA GIỐNG VỚI CODE DƯỚI
-- Tất cả placeId hợp lệ (cũ + mới)
local ValidPlaceIds = {
    2753915549,      -- Sea1 cũ
    4442272183,      -- Sea2 cũ
    7449423635,      -- Sea3 cũ
    85211729168715,  -- Sea1 mới
    79091703265657,  -- Sea2 mới
    100117331123089  -- Sea3 mới
}

-- Xác định Sea (hỗ trợ cả placeId cũ và mới)
local Sea1 = game.PlaceId == 85211729168715 or game.PlaceId == 2753915549
local Sea2 = game.PlaceId == 79091703265657 or game.PlaceId == 4442272183  
local Sea3 = game.PlaceId == 100117331123089 or game.PlaceId == 7449423635

-- Kiểm tra placeId hợp lệ
local IsValidPlace = false
for _, id in ipairs(ValidPlaceIds) do
    if game.PlaceId == id then
        IsValidPlace = true
        break
    end
end

if not IsValidPlace then
    plr:Kick("Unsupport game")
    return
end

-- Xác định World number
local World = (Sea1 and 1) or (Sea2 and 2) or (Sea3 and 3) or false

-- Danh sách cổng từ file Zinner Hub
local EntranceList = {
    {
        ["Sky Arena 1"] = Vector3.new(-4654.3046875, 872.5831909179688, -1759.1536865234375),
        ["Sky Arena 2"] = Vector3.new(-7894.6181640625, 5547.14208984375, -380.2909851074219),
        ["UndeyWater City 1"] = Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 11.680007934570312, 1819.7840576171875),
        ["UndeyWater City 2"] = Vector3.new(3864.68798828125, 5.41372013092041, -1926.2139892578125)
    },
    {
        Mansion = Vector3.new(-288.0594177246094, 305.90118408203125, 613.7842407226562),
        ["Swan Room"] = Vector3.new(2284.96533203125, 15.192192077636719, 897.8220825195312),
        ["Out Ship"] = Vector3.new(-6518.1787109375, 83.2271957397461, -145.9620819091797),
        ["In Ship"] = Vector3.new(923.448486328125, 125.0971908569336, 32883.55859375)
    },
    {
        Mansion = Vector3.new(-12550.44921875, 337.20880126953125, -7476.7158203125),
        ["Castle On The Sea"] = Vector3.new(-5073.84912109375, 314.5505676269531, -3152.5322265625),
        ["Hydra Island"] = Vector3.new(5681.67236328125, 1013.0841674804688, -313.3254699707031),
        ["Temple Of Time"] = Vector3.new(28294.119140625, 14896.5498046875, 103.02708435058594)
    }
}

-- Kiểm tra đánh bại Indra
local indraDefeated = false
spawn(function()
    local success, result = pcall(function()
        local RemoteFunction = ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:WaitForChild("CommF_")
        while wait() do
            local unlockables = RemoteFunction:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables")
            if unlockables and unlockables.DefeatedIndraTrueForm then
                indraDefeated = true
                break
            end
        end
    end)
    if not success then
        -- Nếu không thể kiểm tra, giả sử chưa đánh bại
        indraDefeated = false
    end
end)

local function GetNearEntrance(position)
    local nearestEntrance = nil
    local shortestDistance = math.huge
    
    if not EntranceList[World] then
        return nil
    end
    
    for entranceName, entrancePosition in pairs(EntranceList[World]) do
        local distance = (entrancePosition - position).Magnitude
        if distance < shortestDistance then
            shortestDistance = distance
            nearestEntrance = entrancePosition
        end
    end
    return nearestEntrance
end

local function GetDistance(position1, position2)
    if position2 == nil then
        position2 = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        if not position2 then
            return math.huge
        end
    end
    
    if typeof(position1) == "CFrame" then
        return (position1.Position - position2).Magnitude
    end
    
    if typeof(position1) == "Vector3" then
        return (position1 - position2).Magnitude
    end
    
    return math.huge
end

local function Entranceto(entrancePosition)
    local success, result = pcall(function()
        local RemoteFunction = ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:WaitForChild("CommF_")
        RemoteFunction:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", entrancePosition)
        if plr.Character and plr.Character.PrimaryPart then
            plr.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                plr.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame.X, 
                plr.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame.Y + 120, 
                plr.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame.Z
            )
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end)
    return success
end

local function StopTween()
    if _G.StopTween then
        return
    end
    _G.StopTween = true
    local player = plr
    local character = player.Character
    if character and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
        local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
        end
    end
    if character then
        if character:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
            character.BodyClip:Destroy()
        end
        if character:FindFirstChild("PartTele") then
            character.PartTele:Destroy()
        end
    end
    
    -- Hủy noclip khi dừng tween
    if noclipConnection then
        noclipConnection:Disconnect()
        noclipConnection = nil
    end
    -- Khôi phục collisions
    for part, canCollide in pairs(originalCollisions) do
        if part and part.Parent then
            part.CanCollide = canCollide
        end
    end
    originalCollisions = {}
    
    _G.StopTween = false
end

local function WaitHRP(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then
        return
    end
    return player.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function ToVector3(Pos)
    if typeof(Pos) == "CFrame" then
        return Pos.Position
    elseif typeof(Pos) == "Vector3" then
        return Pos
    elseif typeof(Pos) == "Instance" and Pos:IsA("BasePart") then
        return Pos.Position
    elseif typeof(Pos) == "table" and Pos.x and Pos.y and Pos.z then
        return Vector3.new(Pos.x, Pos.y, Pos.z)
    else
        return nil
    end
end

local function Distance(a, b, noHeight)
    local vector_a = Vector3.new(a.X, not noHeight and a.Y or 0, a.Z)
    local vector_b = Vector3.new(b.X, not noHeight and b.Y or 0, b.Z)
    return (vector_a - vector_b).magnitude
end

-- Hàm noclip
local function EnableNoclip(character)
    if not character then return end
    
    -- Lưu trạng thái collision hiện tại
    originalCollisions = {}
    for _, part in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            originalCollisions[part] = part.CanCollide
            part.CanCollide = false
        end
    end
    
    -- Kết nối để duy trì noclip
    if noclipConnection then
        noclipConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    
    noclipConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not character or not isTeleporting then
            if noclipConnection then
                noclipConnection:Disconnect()
                noclipConnection = nil
            end
            return
        end
        
        for _, part in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Hàm disable noclip
local function DisableNoclip()
    if noclipConnection then
        noclipConnection:Disconnect()
        noclipConnection = nil
    end
    
    -- Khôi phục collisions
    for part, canCollide in pairs(originalCollisions) do
        if part and part.Parent then
            part.CanCollide = canCollide
        end
    end
    originalCollisions = {}
end

function Tween(Pos)
    local vec = ToVector3(Pos)
    if not vec then
        return
    end
    local character = plr.Character
    if character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        if character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        
        local hrp = character.HumanoidRootPart
        local distance = Distance(vec, hrp.Position, true)
        local speed = _G.TweenSpeed -- Sử dụng tốc độ từ dropdown
        
        -- LOGIC SMART TWEEN TỪ ZINNER HUB
        local nearestEntrance = GetNearEntrance(vec)
        
        -- Kiểm tra nếu có cổng gần hơn
        if nearestEntrance and (Sea3 and indraDefeated or Sea2 or Sea1) then
            local distanceToEntrance = (nearestEntrance - vec).Magnitude + 150
            if distanceToEntrance < distance then
                -- Sử dụng cổng
                Entranceto(nearestEntrance)
                task.wait(0.5) -- Chờ sau khi teleport
                
                -- Cập nhật lại vị trí sau khi teleport
                if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    hrp = character.HumanoidRootPart
                    distance = Distance(vec, hrp.Position, true)
                end
            end
        end
        
        -- Logic điều chỉnh tốc độ dựa trên khoảng cách (GIỮ NGUYÊN TỪ BAN ĐẦU)
        if distance < 170 then
            speed = _G.TweenSpeed
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(vec)
            return
        elseif distance < 1000 then
            speed = _G.TweenSpeed
        else
            speed = _G.TweenSpeed
        end
        
        -- Di chuyển lên cao trước khi tween (GIỮ NGUYÊN TỪ BAN ĐẦU)
        if distance > 50 then
            local TOP_CFrame = CFrame.new(vec.X, vec.Y + 300, vec.Z)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, TOP_CFrame.Position.Y, hrp.Position.Z)
        end
        
        -- Tạo PartTele cho tween (GIỮ NGUYÊN TỪ BAN ĐẦU)
        if not character:FindFirstChild("PartTele") then
            local PartTele = Instance.new("Part")
            PartTele.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
            PartTele.Name = "PartTele"
            PartTele.Anchored = true
            PartTele.Transparency = 1
            PartTele.CanCollide = false -- Đặt thành false để tránh va chạm
            PartTele.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
            PartTele.Parent = character
            
            PartTele:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame"):Connect(function()
                if not isTeleporting then
                    return
                end
                task.wait()
                if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    hrp.CFrame = PartTele.CFrame
                end
            end)
        end
        
        -- Kích hoạt noclip trước khi bắt đầu tween (GIỮ NGUYÊN TỪ BAN ĐẦU)
        EnableNoclip(character)
        
        isTeleporting = true
        local partTele = character.PartTele
        partTele.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
        
        local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(distance / speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
        local targetCFrame
        
        -- Nếu khoảng cách lớn hơn 50, tween đến vị trí cao (GIỮ NGUYÊN TỪ BAN ĐẦU)
        if distance > 50 then
            targetCFrame = CFrame.new(vec.X, vec.Y + 300, vec.Z)
        else
            targetCFrame = CFrame.new(vec)
        end
        
        local tween = TweenService:Create(partTele, tweenInfo, {
            CFrame = targetCFrame
        })
        
        tween:Play()
        tween.Completed:Connect(function(status)
            if status == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
                -- Đảm bảo đến đúng vị trí cuối cùng
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(vec)
                
                if character:FindFirstChild("PartTele") then
                    character.PartTele:Destroy()
                end
                
                -- Tắt noclip khi hoàn thành
                DisableNoclip()
                isTeleporting = false
            end
        end)
    end
end

function stopTeleport()
    isTeleporting = false
    local char = plr.Character
    local partTele = char and char:FindFirstChild("PartTele")
    if partTele then
        partTele:Destroy()
    end
    
    -- Tắt noclip khi dừng teleport
    DisableNoclip()
end

local function onCharacterAdded(character)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
    humanoid.Died:Connect(stopTeleport)
    
    -- Đảm bảo noclip được tắt khi character mới spawn
    DisableNoclip()
end

plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded)
if plr.Character then
    onCharacterAdded(plr.Character)
end

    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50))

local hIn = z:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90)
})
local hOut = z:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
})

b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    hIn:Play()
end)
b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    hOut:Play()
end)

b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if typeof(StopTween) == "function" then
        StopTween()
    end
    local c1 = z:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0.96, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.02, 0, 0.02, 0)
    })
    local c2 = z:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    })
    c1:Play()
    c1.Completed:Wait()
    c2:Play()
end)

function Equip(ToolSe)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(ToolSe) then
        local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(ToolSe)
        task.wait()
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
    end
end

function UnEquip(Weapon)
    local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    local backpack = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
    if character and character:FindFirstChild(Weapon) then
        local tool = character:FindFirstChild(Weapon)
        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
            tool.Parent = backpack
        end
    end
end
function CheckMaterial(matname)
    local inventory = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
    for _, v in pairs(inventory) do
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Type == "Material" and v.Name == matname then
            return v.Count
        end
    end
    return 0
end
function GetValuable(name)
    for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")) do
        if v.Name == name then
            return v.Count or v.count or 0
        end
    end
    return 0
end
function GetInventory(Name)
    local inventory = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")

    for i, v in pairs(inventory) do
        if v.Name == Name then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Modules = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules")
local Net = require(Modules:WaitForChild("Net"))
local RegisterAttack = Net:RemoteEvent("RegisterAttack")
local NetModule = require(ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net)

local RegisterHit = NetModule:RemoteEvent("RegisterHit", true)
local CombatFlag = require(Modules:WaitForChild("Flags")).COMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD

local coroutineHit = coroutine.create(function()
    repeat
        task.wait()
    until RegisterHit
    local uid = tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId):sub(2, 4)
    local tid = uid .. tostring(coroutine.running()):sub(11, 15)
    RegisterHit:FireServer(tid)
    while true do
        local mainHit, allHits = coroutine.yield()
        RegisterHit:FireServer(mainHit, allHits, nil, tid)
    end
end)
coroutine.resume(coroutineHit)

_G.SendHitsToServer = function(trigger, mainHit, _, _, fullQueue)
    if trigger and # fullQueue > 0 then
        if CombatFlag then
            coroutine.resume(coroutineHit, mainHit, fullQueue)
        else
            RegisterHit:FireServer(mainHit, fullQueue)
        end
    end
end

local function IsAlive(model)
    local h = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return h and h.Health > 0
end

local function GetTool()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("Tool") then
            return v
        end
    end
end

local function CanAttack()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        return false
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local tool = GetTool()
    if not (hum and hum.Health > 0 and tool) then
        return false
    end
    if hum.Sit and (tool.ToolTip == "Sword" or tool.ToolTip == "Melee" or tool.ToolTip == "Gun" or tool.ToolTip == "Blox Fruit") then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

local enemyParts = {
    "Head",
    "UpperTorso",
    "LowerTorso",
    "HumanoidRootPart",
    "RightHand",
    "LeftHand",
    "RightFoot",
    "LeftFoot"
}

local playerParts = {
    "Head",
    "UpperTorso",
    "LowerTorso",
    "HumanoidRootPart"
}

local function FindTargets(radius)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        return {}
    end
    local origin = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position
    local targets = {}
    local enemies = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies")
    if enemies then
        for _, enemy in ipairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if IsAlive(enemy) then
                local root = enemy:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and (origin - root.Position).Magnitude <= radius then
                    table.insert(targets, {
                        enemy,
                        root,
                        (origin - root.Position).Magnitude
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character and IsAlive(player.Character) then
            local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root and (origin - root.Position).Magnitude <= radius then
                table.insert(targets, {
                    player.Character,
                    root,
                    (origin - root.Position).Magnitude
                })
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(targets, function(a, b)
        return a[3] < b[3]
    end)
    return targets
end

local function DoAttack(mainTarget, queue, tool)
    spawn(function()
        RegisterAttack:FireServer(0.05)
        _G.SendHitsToServer(true, mainTarget, {}, tool, queue)
    end)
end

function Attack()
    if not CanAttack() then
        return
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local tool = GetTool()
    if not tool then
        return
    end
    local targets = FindTargets(90)
    if # targets == 0 then
        return
    end
    local mainTarget = targets[1][2]
    local queue = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(targets) do
        table.insert(queue, {
            t[1],
            t[2]
        })
    end
    spawn(function()
        if tool.ToolTip == "Sword" or tool.ToolTip == "Melee" then
            DoAttack(mainTarget, queue, tool)
        elseif tool.ToolTip == "Blox Fruit" and not _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit then
            local pos = char:GetPivot().Position
            local dir = (mainTarget.Position - pos).Unit
            local remote = tool:FindFirstChild("LeftClickRemote")
            if remote then
                remote:FireServer(dir, 1)
            else
                DoAttack(mainTarget, queue, tool)
            end
            local tap = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteFunction")
            if tap then
                spawn(function()
                    tap:InvokeServer("TAP", mainTarget.Position, pos)
                end)
            end
        elseif tool.ToolTip == "Gun" and not _G.AutoFarmMasGun then
            local remote = tool:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvent")
            if remote then
                for _, t in ipairs(targets) do
                    spawn(function()
                        remote:FireServer("TAP", t[2].Position)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
local X = 0
local Y = 25
local Z = 0
Pos = Vector3.new(X, Y, Z)
function AutoHaki()
    if not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HasBuso") then
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
    end
end

local FarmTypeSelected = ""
local IsAutoFarmEnabled = false
local FarmValues = {
    "Level",
    "Near Mob"
}

if Sea3 then
    table.insert(FarmValues, "Bone")
    table.insert(FarmValues, "Tyrant Skies")
    table.insert(FarmValues, "Cake")
end

local DropdownFarmType = Tabs.Main:AddDropdown("DropdownFarmType", {
    Title = "Select Farm",
    Values = FarmValues,
    Multi = false,
    Default = false
})

DropdownFarmType:SetValue("")

DropdownFarmType:OnChanged(function(Value)
    FarmTypeSelected = Value
    if IsAutoFarmEnabled then
        _G.AutoLevel = false
        _G.AutoNear = false
        _G.AutoBone = false
        _G.Cake = false
        _G.AutoTyrant = false

        _G.AutoLevel = Value == "Level"
        _G.AutoNear = Value == "Near Mob"
        _G.AutoBone = Value == "Bone"
        _G.Cake = Value == "Cake"
        _G.AutoTyrant = Value == "Tyrant Skies"
    end
end)

local ToggleAutoFarm = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleAutoFarm", {
    Title = "Start Farm",
    Description = "",
    Default = false
})

ToggleAutoFarm:OnChanged(function(Value)
    IsAutoFarmEnabled = Value
    if Value then
        _G.AutoLevel = FarmTypeSelected == "Level"
        _G.AutoNear = FarmTypeSelected == "Near Mob"
        _G.AutoBone = FarmTypeSelected == "Bone"
        _G.Cake = FarmTypeSelected == "Cake"
        _G.AutoTyrant = FarmTypeSelected == "Tyrant Skies"
    else
        _G.AutoLevel = false
        _G.AutoNear = false
        _G.AutoBone = false
        _G.Cake = false
        _G.AutoTyrant = false
    end
end)
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoLevel then
            pcall(function()

                CheckQuest()

                local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local questGui = plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest

                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                -- 1) CHƯA CÓ QUEST → ĐI ĐẾN NPC TRƯỚC → RỒI MỚI NHẬN QUEST
                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                if not questGui.Visible then
                    
                    -- di chuyển tới NPC quest
                    if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - CFrameQuest.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                        Tween(CFrameQuest)
                        return
                    end

                    -- đứng đủ gần rồi mới nhận
                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", NameQuest, LevelQuest)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end


                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                -- 2) NẾU ĐANG CÓ QUEST NHƯNG SAI LOẠI → BỎ / NHẬN LẠI
                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                if questGui.Visible then
                    local text = questGui.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text

                    if not string.find(text, Mon) then
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
                        task.wait(0.3)

                        -- đi đến NPC 1 lần nữa cho chắc
                        Tween(CFrameQuest)
                        task.wait(0.2)

                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", NameQuest, LevelQuest)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end


                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                -- 3) TÌM QUÁI ĐÚNG
                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                local target = nil
                for _, v in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if v.Name == Mon and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                        target = v
                        break
                    end
                end


                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                -- 4) KHÔNG CÓ QUÁI → ĐI ĐẾN CFRAMEMON
                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                if not target then
                    Tween(CFrameMon)
                    return
                end


                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                -- 5) ĐÁNH QUÁI – THEO FORMAT BẠN MUỐN
                ----------------------------------------------------------------
                repeat task.wait()

	                    Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                until not _G.AutoLevel or not target.Parent or target.Humanoid.Health <= 0

            end)
        end
    end
end)
local QuestCFrame = CFrame.new(-2020.87,37.78,-12027.88)

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.Cake then
            pcall(function()
                local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local char = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local QuestUI = plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest

                -- === ƯU TIÊN BOSS ===
                local boss = game.Workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Cake Prince")
                    or game.Workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Dough King")

                if boss and boss:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    and boss:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    and boss.Humanoid.Health > 0 then

                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(boss.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)
                        FarmPos = boss.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        MonFarm = boss.Name
                    until not _G.Cake or not boss.Parent or boss.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                end

                -- === CHỈ NHẬN QUEST KHI _G.Acceptquest = true ===
                if QuestUI.Visible then
                    if not string.find(QuestUI.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "Cookie Crafter") then
                        if _G.Acceptquest then
                            game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
                            task.wait(0.7)
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- === NHẬN QUEST NẾU ĐƯỢC BẬT ===
                if _G.Acceptquest and not QuestUI.Visible then
                    Tween(QuestCFrame)
                    repeat task.wait() until (hrp.Position - QuestCFrame.Position).Magnitude < 10
                    task.wait(0.5)

                    game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", "CakeQuest1", 1)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end

                -- === FARM DÙ CÓ QUEST HAY KHÔNG ===
                local target
                local closest = math.huge

                for _, v in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then

                        if v.Name == "Baking Staff" or v.Name == "Head Baker" or v.Name == "Cake Guard" or v.Name == "Cookie Crafter" then
                            local dist = (v.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < closest then
                                closest = dist
                                target = v
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if target then
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)
                        FarmPos = target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        MonFarm = target.Name
                    until not _G.Cake or not target.Parent or target.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                else
                    Tween(QuestCFrame)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoNear then
            pcall(function()
                for _, v in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                        if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - v.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10000 then
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                Attack()
			                    AutoHaki()
                                Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                                FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                MonFarm = v.Name
                            until not _G.AutoNear or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not game.Workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

    while task.wait() do

                            game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
                            task.wait(0.7)
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(1.5)

                    game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", "HauntedQuest2", 2)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end

                -- Farm dù có quest hay không
                local target
                local closest = math.huge

                for _, v in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then

                        if v.Name == "Reborn Skeleton"
                            or v.Name == "Living Zombie"
                            or v.Name == "Demonic Soul"
                            or v.Name == "Posessed Mummy" then

                            local dist = (v.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < closest then
                                closest = dist
                                target = v
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if target then
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)
                        FarmPos = target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        MonFarm = target.Name
                    until not _G.AutoBone or not target.Parent or target.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                else
                    Tween(BoneCFrame)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

        while task.wait() do
            if _G.CastleRaid then
                pcall(function()
                    local CFrameCastleRaid = CFrame.new(- 5496.17432, 313.768921, - 2841.53027, 0.924894512, 7.37058015e-09, 0.380223751, 3.5881019e-08, 1, - 1.06665446e-07, - 0.380223751, 1.12297109e-07, 0.924894512)
                    if (CFrame.new(- 5539.3115234375, 313.800537109375, - 2972.372314453125).Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 500 then
                        for i, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if _G.CastleRaid and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                                if (v.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 2000 then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        Attack()
                                        AutoHaki()
                                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                        MonFarm = v.Name
                                        FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                        Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                                    until v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v.Parent or not _G.CastleRaid
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        Tween(CFrameCastleRaid)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    local ToggleAutoRipIndra = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleAutoRipIndra", {
        Title = "Auto Activate Color (Rip Indra)",
        Description = "",
        Default = false
    })
    ToggleAutoRipIndra:OnChanged(function(Value)
        _G.AutoRipIndra = Value
    end)
    local hakiMap = {
        ["Oyster"] = "Snow White",
        ["Really red"] = "Pure Red",
        ["Hot pink"] = "Winter Sky"
    }
    spawn(function()
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local RF = ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net["RF/FruitCustomizerRF"]
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if not _G.AutoRipIndra then
                    return
                end
                local hrp = Players.LocalPlayer.Character and Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then
                    return
                end
                local circle = Workspace.Map["Boat Castle"].Summoner.Circle
                local handled = {}
                for _, part in ipairs(circle:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("Part") and part:FindFirstChild("Part") and part.Part.BrickColor.Name == "Dark stone grey" and not table.find(handled, part) then
                        local haki = hakiMap[part.BrickColor.Name]
                        if haki then
                            RF:InvokeServer({
                                StorageName = haki,
                                Type = "AuraSkin",
                                Context = "Equip"
                            })
                        end
                        Tween(CFrame.new(part.Position))
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                        until not _G.AutoRipIndra or _G.StopTween or (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude <= 5
                        if part:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
                            firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 1)
                        end
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                        until not _G.AutoRipIndra or not (part:IsDescendantOf(circle) and part:FindFirstChild("Part") and part.Part.BrickColor.Name == "Dark stone grey")
                        table.insert(handled, part)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end
local Chest = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("Chest", {
    Title = "Collect Chest",
    Description = "",
    Default = false
})
Chest:OnChanged(function(Value)
    _G.AutoFarmChest = Value
end)
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoFarmChest then
            pcall(function()
                local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
                local Players = game:GetService("Players")
                local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
                local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                if not Character then
                    return
                end
                local Position = Character:GetPivot().Position
                local Chests = CollectionService:GetTagged("_ChestTagged")
                local Distance, Nearest = math.huge, nil
                for i = 1, # Chests do
                    local Chest = Chests[i]
                    local Magnitude = (Chest:GetPivot().Position - Position).Magnitude
                    if not SelectedIsland or Chest:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland) then
                        if not Chest:GetAttribute("IsDisabled") and Magnitude < Distance then
                            Distance = Magnitude
                            Nearest = Chest
                        end
                    end
                end
                if Nearest then
                    Tween(Nearest:GetPivot())
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
local Berry = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("Berry", {
    Title = "Collect Berry",
    Description = "",
    Default = false
})

Berry:OnChanged(function(Value)
    _G.AutoBerry = Value
end)

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoBerry then
            local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
            local BerryBush = CollectionService:GetTagged("BerryBush")

            local Distance, Nearest = math.huge

            for i = 1, # BerryBush do
                local Bush = BerryBush[i]

                for AttributeName, BerryName in pairs(Bush:GetAttributes()) do
                    if not BerryArray or table.find(BerryArray, BerryName) then
                        Tween(Bush.Parent:GetPivot())

                        for j = 1, # BerryBush do
                            local SubBush = BerryBush[j]

                            for _, Child in pairs(SubBush:GetChildren()) do
                                if not BerryArray or table.find(BerryArray, Child.Name) then
                                    Tween(Child.WorldPivot)
                                    fireproximityprompt(Child.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
if Sea2 or Sea3 then
    local DropdownMastery = Tabs.Main:AddDropdown("DropdownMastery", {
        Title = "Select Island Farm",
        Description = "",
        Values = {},
        Multi = false,
    })
    local function UpdateDropdownValues()
        if Sea3 then
            DropdownMastery:SetValues({
                "Level",
                "Near Mob",
                "Bone",
                "Cake"
            })
        elseif Sea2 then
            DropdownMastery:SetValues({
                "Level",
                "Near Mob",
                "Ecto"
            })
        else
            DropdownMastery:SetValues({})
        end
    end
    UpdateDropdownValues()
    if TypeMastery then
        DropdownMastery:SetValue(TypeMastery)
    end
    DropdownMastery:OnChanged(function(Value)
        TypeMastery = Value
    end)
    local ToggleZ = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleZ", {
        Title = "Skill Z",
        Description = "",
        Default = true
    })
    ToggleZ:OnChanged(function(Value)
        SkillZ = Value
    end)
    local ToggleX = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleX", {
        Title = "Skill X",
        Description = "",
        Default = true
    })
    ToggleX:OnChanged(function(Value)
        SkillX = Value
    end)
    local ToggleC = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleC", {
        Title = "Skill C",
        Description = "",
        Default = true
    })
    ToggleC:OnChanged(function(Value)
        SkillC = Value
    end)
    local ToggleV = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleV", {
        Title = "Skill V",
        Description = "",
        Default = true
    })
    ToggleV:OnChanged(function(Value)
        SkillV = Value
    end)
    local ToggleF = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleF", {
        Title = "Skill F",
        Description = "",
        Default = true
    })
    ToggleF:OnChanged(function(Value)
        SkillF = Value
    end)
    local MasterySelected = ""
    local IsMasteryFarmEnabled = false
    _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit = false
    _G.AutoFarmMasGun = false
    local DropdownFarmMastery = Tabs.Main:AddDropdown("DropdownFarmMastery", {
        Title = "Select Farm Mastery",
        Values = {
            "Fruit",
            "Gun"
        },
        Multi = false,
        Default = false
    })
    DropdownFarmMastery:SetValue("")
    DropdownFarmMastery:OnChanged(function(Value)
        MasterySelected = Value
        if IsMasteryFarmEnabled then
            if Value == "Fruit" then
                _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit = true
                _G.AutoFarmMasGun = false
            elseif Value == "Gun" then
                _G.AutoFarmMasGun = true
                _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit = false
            end
        end
    end)
    local ToggleFarmMastery = Tabs.Main:AddToggle("ToggleFarmMastery", {
        Title = "Mastery Farm",
        Description = "",
        Default = false
    })
    ToggleFarmMastery:OnChanged(function(Value)
        IsMasteryFarmEnabled = Value
        if Value then
            if MasterySelected == "Fruit" then
                _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit = true
                _G.AutoFarmMasGun = false
            elseif MasterySelected == "Gun" then
                _G.AutoFarmMasGun = true
                _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit = false
            end
        else
            _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit = false
            _G.AutoFarmMasGun = false
        end
    end)
    local InputHealth = Tabs.Main:AddInput("InputHealth", {
        Title = "Health Mob",
        Default = "40",
        Placeholder = "",
        Numeric = true,
        Finished = true,
        Callback = function(Value)
            KillPercent = tonumber(Value)
        end
    })
    InputHealth:OnChanged(function()
        KillPercent = tonumber(InputHealth.Value)
    end)
end
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.UseSkill then
            pcall(function()
                for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if v.Name == MonFarm and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Humanoid.Health <= v.Humanoid.MaxHealth * KillPercent / 100 then
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            EquipFruit()
                            Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                            PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit = v.HumanoidRootPart.Position 

                            if SkillZ then
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
                                task.wait()
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
                            end
                            if SkillX then
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
                                task.wait()
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
                            end
                            if SkillC then
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game)
                                task.wait()
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game)
                            end
                            if SkillV then
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, "V", false, game)
                                task.wait()
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, "V", false, game)
                            end
                            if SkillF then
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, "F", false, game)
                                task.wait()
                                game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, "F", false, game)
                            end
                        until not _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit or not _G.UseSkill or v.Humanoid.Health == 0
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit and TypeMastery == 'Near Mob' then
            pcall(function()
                for i, v in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                        local distance = (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - v.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                        if distance <= 5000 then
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                if not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                                    break
                                end

                                if v.Humanoid.Health <= v.Humanoid.MaxHealth * KillPercent / 100 then
                                    _G.UseSkill = true
                                else
                                    _G.UseSkill = false
                                    AutoHaki()
                                    Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                    Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                                    FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                    MonFarm = v.Name
                                    Attack()
                                end
                            until not _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit or MasteryType ~= 'Near Mob' or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or TypeMastery ~= 'Near Mob'
                            
                            _G.UseSkill = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

            pcall(function()
                CheckLevel()

                local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
                local gui = player.PlayerGui.Main.Quest
                local questTitle = gui.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text

                if not string.find(questTitle) or gui.Visible == false then
                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", NameQuest, QuestLv)
                end

                if string.find(questTitle) and gui.Visible then
                    local FoundEnemy = false

                    for i, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Name == Monster then
                            FoundEnemy = true
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                if not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                                    break
                                end

                                if v.Humanoid.Health <= v.Humanoid.MaxHealth * KillPercent / 100 then
                                    _G.UseSkill = true
                                else
                                    _G.UseSkill = false
                                    AutoHaki()
                                    Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                    Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                                    FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                    MonFarm = v.Name
                                    Attack()
                                end
                            until not _G.AutoFarmMasDevilFruit or MasteryType ~= "Level" or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0

                            _G.UseSkill = false
                        end
                    end

                    if not FoundEnemy then
                        Tween(CFrameMonster)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
function EquipGun()
    pcall(function()
        for _, v in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Tool") and v.ToolTip == "Gun" then
                game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v)
                break
            end
        end
    end)
end

function EquipFruit()
    pcall(function()
        for _, v in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("Tool") and v.ToolTip == "Blox Fruit" then
                game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v)
                break
            end
        end
    end)
end
spawn(function()
    local gg = getrawmetatable(game)
    local old = gg.__namecall
    setreadonly(gg, false)

    gg.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
        local method = getnamecallmethod()
        local args = {
            ...
        }

        if tostring(method) == "FireServer" then
            if tostring(args[1]) == "RemoteEvent" then
                if tostring(args[2]) ~= "true" and tostring(args[2]) ~= "false" then
                    if _G.UseSkillGun then
                        if typeof(args[2]) == "Vector3" then
                            args[2] = PositionSkillMasteryGun
                        else
                            args[2] = CFrame.new(PositionSkillMasteryGun)
                        end
                        return old(unpack(args))
                    end

                    if _G.UseSkill then
                        if typeof(args[2]) == "Vector3" then
                            args[2] = PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit
                        else
                            args[2] = CFrame.new(PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit)
                        end
                        return old(unpack(args))
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return old(...)
    end)

    setreadonly(gg, true)
end)

local function getAvailableBosses()
    local available = {}
    for _, boss in pairs(tableBoss) do
        local inWorkspace = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:FindFirstChild(boss)
        local inReplicated = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild(boss)
        if inWorkspace or inReplicated then
            table.insert(available, boss)
        end
    end
    return available
end

local currentBossList = getAvailableBosses()
local DropdownBoss = Tabs.Main:AddDropdown("DropdownBoss", {
    Title = "Select Boss",
    Description = "",
    Values = currentBossList,
    Multi = false,
    Default = false
})
_G.SelectBoss = nil

DropdownBoss:SetValue(_G.SelectBoss)
DropdownBoss:OnChanged(function(Value)
    _G.SelectBoss = Value
end)

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        local newBossList = getAvailableBosses()
        local changed = false
        if # newBossList ~= # currentBossList then
            changed = true
        else
            for i = 1, # newBossList do
                if newBossList[i] ~= currentBossList[i] then
                    changed = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if changed then
            currentBossList = newBossList
            DropdownBoss:SetValues(currentBossList)
            if not table.find(currentBossList, _G.SelectBoss) then
                _G.SelectBoss = nil
                DropdownBoss:SetValue(nil)
            end
        end
    end
end)

local currentTarget = nil

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoBoss and _G.SelectBoss then
            if currentTarget ~= _G.SelectBoss then
                currentTarget = _G.SelectBoss
            end

            pcall(function()
                local boss = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:FindFirstChild(currentTarget)
                if boss then
                    for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if v.Name == currentTarget and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                            repeat
                                if not _G.AutoBoss or _G.SelectBoss ~= currentTarget then
                                    break
                                end
                                task.wait()
                                Attack()
                                AutoHaki()
                                Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                                sethiddenproperty(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
                            until not _G.AutoBoss or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or _G.SelectBoss ~= currentTarget
                        end
                    end
                else
                    local storedBoss = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild(currentTarget)
                    if storedBoss and storedBoss:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        Tween(storedBoss.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            currentTarget = nil
        end
    end
end)

        if game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Kitsune Island') then
            StatusKitsune:SetDesc("✅️")
        else
            StatusKitsune:SetDesc("❌️")

            end

    local ToggleAutoKitsune = Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("ToggleAutoKitsune", {
        Title = "Auto Complete Kitsune",
        Description = "",
        Default = false
    })
    ToggleAutoKitsune:OnChanged(function(Value)
        _G.AutoKitsune = Value
    end)
    spawn(function()
        local kitsuneIsland
        repeat
            kitsuneIsland = game:GetService("Workspace"):WaitForChild("Map", 5):FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland")
            task.wait()
        until kitsuneIsland
        local KitsuneDone = false
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoKitsune then
                for _, obj in ipairs(kitsuneIsland:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and obj.ActionText == "..." and obj.ObjectText == "Kitsune Shrine" then
                        local parentPart = obj.Parent
                        if parentPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                            Tween(parentPart.CFrame)
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                            until (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - parentPart.Position).Magnitude <= 5
                            fireproximityprompt(obj, obj.HoldDuration)
                            local touchEvent = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RE/TouchKitsuneStatue")
                            if touchEvent then
                                touchEvent:FireServer()
                            end
                            KitsuneDone = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                local collectEvent = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RE/CollectBlueEmber")
                if collectEvent then
                    collectEvent:FireServer()
                end
                if CheckMaterial("Azure Ember") == 25 then
                    local modules = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules", 5)
                    if modules then
                        local net = modules:FindFirstChild("Net")
                        if net then
                            local tradeFunction = net:FindFirstChild("RF/KitsuneStatuePray")
                            if tradeFunction then
                                tradeFunction:InvokeServer()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

    local function getIslandConfig(name)

            Prehistoric = {
                Delete = islandsToDelete.Prehistoric,
                Target = "PrehistoricIsland",
                Notify = "Found Prehistoric Island"
            },
            Mirage = {
                Delete = islandsToDelete.Mirage,
                Target = "MysticIsland",

            Frozen = {
                Delete = islandsToDelete.Frozen,
                Target = "LeviathanGate",
                Notify = "Found Frozen Dimension"
            },
            Kitsune = {
                Delete = islandsToDelete.Kitsune,
                Target = "KitsuneIsland",
                Notify = "Found Kitsune Island"
            }
        }
        return config[name]
    end
    local isBoatTeleporting = false
    local function AddEsp(Name, Parent)
        local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        local TitleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        local DistanceLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        BillboardGui.Parent = Parent
        BillboardGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        BillboardGui.Active = true
        BillboardGui.Name = Name
        BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        BillboardGui.LightInfluence = 0
        BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 40)
        BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0)
        TitleLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
        TitleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        TitleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        TitleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
        TitleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        TitleLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 255)
        TitleLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6
        TitleLabel.TextSize = 14
        TitleLabel.Text = "My Boat"
        DistanceLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
        DistanceLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        DistanceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
        DistanceLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
        DistanceLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        DistanceLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        DistanceLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
        DistanceLabel.TextSize = 13
        DistanceLabel.Text = "..."
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
        local seat = Parent:FindFirstChild("Seat") or Parent:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") or Parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        local function getLocalHRP()
            local char = lp.Character
            return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        local function updateDistance()
            local localPart = getLocalHRP()
            if seat and localPart then
                local dist = math.floor((seat.Position - localPart.Position).Magnitude / 12)
                DistanceLabel.Text = tostring(dist) .. " m"
            else
                DistanceLabel.Text = "N/A"
            end
        end
        game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(updateDistance)
    end
    local function teleportToBoat()
        if isBoatTeleporting then
            return
        end
        isBoatTeleporting = true
        for _, boat in pairs(Workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
            local owner = boat:FindFirstChild("Owner")
            if owner and owner:IsA("ObjectValue") and owner.Value == player then
                local seat = boat:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
                if seat and not seat.Occupant then
                    AddEsp("MyBoatESP", boat)
                    Tween(seat.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        isBoatTeleporting = false
    end
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        character = char
        notified = false
    end)

    })
    Tabs.Sea:AddButton({
        Title = "TP To Area Hunt",
        Callback = function()
            Tween(CFrame.new(- 16917.154, 7.757, 511.820))
        end
    })
    local function handleBoatMovement(seat)
        local scaledDist = math.floor((seat.Position - npcPosition).Magnitude / 10)
        local distSegment = math.floor(scaledDist / 3000)
        local currentPos = seat.Position
        if math.abs(currentPos.Y - 170) > 5 then
            local flatLook = Vector3.new(seat.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, seat.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
            seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(currentPos.X, 170, currentPos.Z), Vector3.new(currentPos.X, 170, currentPos.Z) + flatLook)
        end
        local function rotateLookVector(pattern, index, angleLeft, angleRight)
            local turn = pattern[index]
            local lookVector = seat.CFrame.LookVector
            if turn == "Left" then
                lookVector = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(- angleLeft), 0):VectorToWorldSpace(lookVector)
            elseif turn == "Right" then
                lookVector = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(angleRight), 0):VectorToWorldSpace(lookVector)
            end
            local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z).Unit
            seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(seat.Position, seat.Position + flatLook)
        end
        if IslandTypeSelected == "Frozen" then
            if distSegment ~= lastCheckDistance then
                lastCheckDistance = distSegment
                rotateLookVector(turnPatternFrozen, turnIndexFrozen, 35, 35)
                turnIndexFrozen = turnIndexFrozen % # turnPatternFrozen + 1
            end
        elseif IslandTypeSelected == "Prehistoric" then
            if distSegment ~= lastCheckDistance then
                lastCheckDistance = distSegment
                rotateLookVector(turnPatternPrehistoric, turnIndexPrehistoric, 35, 35)
                turnIndexPrehistoric = turnIndexPrehistoric % # turnPatternPrehistoric + 1
            end
        elseif IslandTypeSelected == "Mirage" or IslandTypeSelected == "Kitsune" then
            local lookVector = seat.CFrame.LookVector
            local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z).Unit
            seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(seat.Position, seat.Position + flatLook)
        end
    end
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then
            return
        end
        local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        currentIsland = nil
        if map and rootPart then
            for _, islandObj in ipairs(map:GetChildren()) do
                if islandObj:IsA("Model") then
                    local cf, size = islandObj:GetBoundingBox()
                    local relPos = cf:PointToObjectSpace(rootPart.Position)
                    if math.abs(relPos.X) <= size.X / 2 and math.abs(relPos.Y) <= size.Y / 2 and math.abs(relPos.Z) <= size.Z / 2 then
                        currentIsland = islandObj.Name
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local blacklist = {
            LeviathanGate = true,
            PrehistoricIsland = true,
            MysticIsland = true,
            KitsuneIsland = true
        }
        if _G.AutoFindIsland and (IslandTypeSelected == "Mirage" or IslandTypeSelected == "Kitsune" or IslandTypeSelected == "Prehistoric") then
            if currentIsland and currentIsland ~= lastIsland and not blacklist[currentIsland] then
                lastIsland = currentIsland
                local targetPos = CFrame.new(- 16917.154, 7.757, 511.820)
                task.wait(1)
                Tween(targetPos)
                repeat
                    task.wait()
                until (character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - targetPos.Position).Magnitude <= 5)
                if not boatBought then
                    buyBoat("Guardian")
                    boatBought = true
                end
                teleportToBoat()
            elseif not currentIsland then
                lastIsland = nil
                autoStarted = false
                boatBought = false
            end
        end
        if _G.AutoFindIsland then
            DestroyEnemyBoats()
        end
        if not _G.AutoFindIsland then
            return
        end
        local config = getIslandConfig(IslandTypeSelected)
        if not config then
            return
        end
        for _, boat in pairs(Workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
            local owner = boat:FindFirstChild("Owner")
            if owner and owner.Value == player then
                local seat = boat:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
                if seat then
                    seat.MaxSpeed = SetSpeedBoat
                    if seat.Occupant == character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                        handleBoatMovement(seat)
                        seat.ThrottleFloat = 1
                        for _, islandName in ipairs(config.Delete) do
                            local island = Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild(islandName)
                            if island then
                                island:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                        local targetIsland = Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild(config.Target)
                        if targetIsland and not notified then
                            local islandPos = targetIsland:GetModelCFrame().Position
                            local boatPos = seat.Position
                            local lookVector = (islandPos - boatPos).Unit
                            seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(boatPos, boatPos + lookVector)
                            seat.ThrottleFloat = 0
                            _G.AutoFindIsland = false
                            Fluent:Notify({
                                Title = "OK Hub",
                                Content = config.Notify,
                                Duration = 10
                            })
                            notified = true
                            autoStarted = false
                        end
                    else
                        teleportToBoat()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
if Sea3 then
    local FarmSeaTypeSelected = {}
    local IsAutoFarmSeaEnabled = false
    local FarmSeaValues = {
        "Shark",
        "FishCrew",
        "Ship",
        "TerrorShark",

    }

        Values = FarmSeaValues,
        Multi = true,
        Default = {},
    })

        FarmSeaTypeSelected = {}
        for Type, Enabled in pairs(Value) do

                table.insert(FarmSeaTypeSelected, Type)
            end
        end
        if IsAutoFarmSeaEnabled then
            _G.AutoTerrorshark = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "TerrorShark") ~= nil
            _G.Piranha = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "Piranha") ~= nil
            _G.Shark = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "Shark") ~= nil
            _G.FishCrew = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "FishCrew") ~= nil
            _G.Ship = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "Ship") ~= nil
        end
    end)
    local ToggleAutoFarmSea = Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("ToggleAutoFarmSea", {
        Title = "Auto Farm Sea Events",
        Description = "",
        Default = false
    })
    ToggleAutoFarmSea:OnChanged(function(Value)
        IsAutoFarmSeaEnabled = Value
        if Value then
            _G.AutoTerrorshark = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "TerrorShark") ~= nil
            _G.Piranha = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "Piranha") ~= nil
            _G.Shark = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "Shark") ~= nil
            _G.FishCrew = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "FishCrew") ~= nil
            _G.Ship = table.find(FarmSeaTypeSelected, "Ship") ~= nil
        else
            _G.AutoTerrorshark = false
            _G.Piranha = false
            _G.Shark = false
            _G.FishCrew = false
            _G.Ship = false
        end
    end)
    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoTerrorshark then
                pcall(function()
                    local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                    if character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local humanoid = character.Humanoid
                        local hrp = character.HumanoidRootPart
                        local heightOffset = humanoid.Health < 6000 and 800 or 30
                        local enemies = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies
                        if enemies:FindFirstChild("Terrorshark") then
                            for _, enemy in pairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                if enemy.Name == "Terrorshark" and enemy:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and enemy:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        Attack()
                                        AutoHaki()
                                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                        Tween(enemy.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, heightOffset, 0))
                                    until not _G.AutoTerrorshark or not enemy.Parent or enemy.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Terrorshark")
                            if rep and rep:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                Tween(rep.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, heightOffset, 0))
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function CheckPirateBoat()
    local checkNames = {
        "PirateGrandBrigade",
        "PirateBrigade"
    }
    for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
        if table.find(checkNames, v.Name) and v:FindFirstChild("Health") and v.Health.Value > 0 then
            return v
        end
    end
end
function CheckGhostBoat()
    for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
        if v.Name == "FishBoat" and v:FindFirstChild("Health") and v.Health.Value > 0 then
            return v
        end
    end
end
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.Ship then
            pcall(function()
                local v = CheckPirateBoat()
                if v then
                    game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                    task.wait()
                    game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Tween(v.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, - 20, 0))
                        AimBotSkillPosition = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, - 5, 0)
                        Skillaimbot = true
                        AutoSkill = false
                    until not _G.Ship or not v.Parent or v.Health.Value <= 0
                    Skillaimbot = false
                    AutoSkill = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.GhostShip then
            pcall(function()
                local v = CheckGhostBoat()
                if v then
                    AutoHaki()
                    local function AttackWithType(tooltip)
                        for _, tool in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                            if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool.ToolTip == tooltip then
                                game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        local keys = {
                            Enum.KeyCode.Z,
                            Enum.KeyCode.X,
                            Enum.KeyCode.C
                        }
                        for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
                            game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, key, false, game)
                            task.wait()
                            game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, key, false, game)
                            task.wait()
                        end
                    end
                    AttackWithType("Melee")
                    AttackWithType("Blox Fruit")
                    game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.V, false, game)
                    task.wait()
                    game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.V, false, game)
                    AttackWithType("Sword")
                    AttackWithType("Gun")
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
