if Sea3 then
    Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("AutoBlazeEmber", {
        Title = "Blaze Ember",
        Description = "",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(toggleValue)
            _G.AutoFarmBlazeEmber = toggleValue
        end
    })
    function getBlazeEmberQuest()
        local res = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/DragonHunter"]:InvokeServer({
            Context = "Check"
        })
        if res then
            for k, v in pairs(res) do
                if k == "Text" then
                    return v
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function getRequestQuest()
        return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/DragonHunter"]:InvokeServer({
            Context = "RequestQuest"
        })
    end
    function getIsOnQuest()
        local res = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RF/DragonHunter"]:InvokeServer({
            Context = "Check"
        })
        if res then
            for k, v in pairs(res) do
                if k == "Text" and (string.find(v, "Venomous Assailant") or string.find(v, "Hydra Enforcer") or string.find(v, "Destroy 10 trees")) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    function SaveBlazeEmberQuest()
        local quest = getBlazeEmberQuest()
        if string.find(quest, "Venomous Assailant") then
            _G.BlazeEmberQuest = "Venomous Assailant"
        elseif string.find(quest, "Hydra Enforcer") then
            _G.BlazeEmberQuest = "Hydra Enforcer"
        elseif string.find(quest, "Destroy 10 trees") then
            _G.BlazeEmberQuest = "Destroy 10 trees"
        end
    end
    function isQuestCompleated()
        for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren()) do
            for _, notif in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
                if string.find(notif.Text, "Task completed!") or string.find(notif.Text, "Head back to the Dojo") then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    function CollectBlazeEmber()
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net["RE/DragonDojoEmber"]:FireServer()
    end
    function autoKillVenemousAssailant()
        local enemies = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies
        if not enemies:FindFirstChild("Venomous Assailant") then
            Tween(CFrame.new(4789.29639, 1078.59082, 962.764099))
        else
            for _, v in pairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                if v.Name == "Venomous Assailant" and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                        FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        MonFarm = v.Name
                    until not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.AutoFarmBlazeEmber or not _G.OnBlzeQuest
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function autoKillHydraEnforcer()
        local enemies = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies
        if not enemies:FindFirstChild("Hydra Enforcer") then
            Tween(CFrame.new(4789.29639, 1078.59082, 962.764099))
        else
            for _, v in pairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                if v.Name == "Hydra Enforcer" and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                        FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        MonFarm = v.Name
                    until not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.AutoFarmBlazeEmber or not _G.OnBlzeQuest
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function autoDestroyHydraTrees()
        local posList = {
            CFrame.new(5260.28223, 1004.24329, 347.062622),
            CFrame.new(5237.94775, 1004.24329, 429.596344),
            CFrame.new(5320.87793, 1004.24329, 439.152954),
            CFrame.new(5346.70752, 1004.24329, 359.389008),
        }
        local myPos = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position
        for _, pos in ipairs(posList) do
            if (myPos - pos.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
                useAllSkill()
            else
                Tween(pos)
            end
        end
    end
    _G.OnBlzeQuest = false
    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoFarmBlazeEmber then
                pcall(function()
                    if not _G.OnBlzeQuest and not getIsOnQuest() then
                        Tween(CFrame.new(5864.86377, 1209.55066, 812.775024))
                        getRequestQuest()
                    end
                    SaveBlazeEmberQuest()
                    _G.OnBlzeQuest = true
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if isQuestCompleated() then
                _G.OnBlzeQuest = false
            end
        end
    end)
    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoFarmBlazeEmber then
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.BlazeEmberQuest == "Venomous Assailant" and _G.OnBlzeQuest then
                        autoKillVenemousAssailant()
                    elseif _G.BlazeEmberQuest == "Hydra Enforcer" and _G.OnBlzeQuest then
                        autoKillHydraEnforcer()
                    elseif _G.BlazeEmberQuest == "Destroy 10 trees" and _G.OnBlzeQuest then
                        autoDestroyHydraTrees()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoFarmBlazeEmber then
            pcall(function()
                CollectBlazeEmber()
            end)
        end
    end
end)

    local DistanceParagraph = Tabs.Status:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Distance Npc Tiki",
        Content = ""
    })

    local npcPosition = Vector3.new(- 16665.191, 104.596, 1579.694)
    local notified = false

    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            local _ok, _err = pcall(function()
                local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local distToNpc = (char.HumanoidRootPart.Position - npcPosition).Magnitude
                    local scaledDist = math.floor(distToNpc / 10)
                    DistanceParagraph:SetDesc(string.format("%d m", scaledDist))

                    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                    local hasIsland = map and (
                        map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") or map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") or map:FindFirstChild("LeviathanGate") or map:FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland"))

                    if scaledDist >= 20000 and not hasIsland then
                        if not notified then
                            Fluent:Notify({
                                Title = "OK Hub",
                                Content = "You have traveled 20,000m. The island may not spawn. If you're in a VIP server, try server hopping or resetting, then try again.",
                                Duration = 10
                            })
                            notified = true
                        end
                    else
                        notified = false
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local TyrantSkiesStatus = Tabs.Status:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Tyrant Of The Skies",
        Content = ""
    })

    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            local _ok, _err = pcall(function()
                local tyrant = workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies") and workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies")
                if tyrant then
                    TyrantSkiesStatus:SetDesc("✅")
                else
                    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                    local count = 0

                    if map then
                        local tikiOutpost = map:FindFirstChild("TikiOutpost")
                        if tikiOutpost then
                            local islandModel = tikiOutpost:FindFirstChild("IslandModel")
                            local islandChunks = islandModel and islandModel:FindFirstChild("IslandChunks") and islandModel.IslandChunks:FindFirstChild("E")

                            if islandModel then
                                for i = 1, 2 do
                                    local eye = islandModel:FindFirstChild("Eye" .. i)
                                    if eye and eye:IsA("BasePart") and eye.Transparency == 0 then
                                        count = count + 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            if islandChunks then
                                for i = 3, 4 do
                                    local eye = islandChunks:FindFirstChild("Eye" .. i)
                                    if eye and eye:IsA("BasePart") and eye.Transparency == 0 then
                                        count = count + 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            TyrantSkiesStatus:SetDesc(count .. "/4")
                        else
                            TyrantSkiesStatus:SetDesc("0/4")
                        end
                    else
                        TyrantSkiesStatus:SetDesc("0/4")
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local BeltInfo = Tabs.Status:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Dojo Belt",
        Content = ""
    })

    local BeltOrder = {
        "Dojo Belt (White)",
        "Dojo Belt (Yellow)",
        "Dojo Belt (Orange)",
        "Dojo Belt (Green)",
        "Dojo Belt (Blue)",
        "Dojo Belt (Purple)",
        "Dojo Belt (Red)",
        "Dojo Belt (Black)"

    local BeltTasks = {
        {
            progress = "Dojo Progress : Get Dojo Belt (White)",
            guide = "Kill 20 NPC on Quest."
        },
        {
            progress = "Dojo Progress : Get Dojo Belt (Yellow)",
            guide = "Kill 5 Sea Mob."
        },

            guide = "Complete Prehistoric Island event and collect 3 Dinosaur bones."
        }

    local function HasBelt(remoteFunction, beltName)
        local inventory = remoteFunction("getInventory")
        for _, item in pairs(inventory or {}) do
            if type(item) == "table" and item.Type == "Wear" and item.Name == beltName then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    spawn(function()
        local remoteFunction = function(cmd)
            return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(cmd)
        end

        while task.wait() do
            local _ok, _err = pcall(function()
                local currentBelt = nil
                local nextGuide = "Next: " .. BeltOrder[1] .. " | Guide: " .. BeltTasks[1].guide

                for i = # BeltOrder, 1, - 1 do
                    if HasBelt(remoteFunction, BeltOrder[i]) then
                        currentBelt = BeltOrder[i]
                        if BeltOrder[i + 1] then
                            nextGuide = "Next: " .. BeltOrder[i + 1] .. " | Guide: " .. BeltTasks[i + 1].guide
                        else
                            nextGuide = "You have all belts!"
                        end
                        break
                    end
                end

                if nextGuide == "You have all belts!" then
                    BeltInfo:SetDesc(nextGuide)
                elseif currentBelt then
                    BeltInfo:SetDesc(currentBelt .. " | " .. nextGuide)
                else
                    BeltInfo:SetDesc(nextGuide)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

    local ToggleTPVolcano = Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("ToggleTPVolcano", { 
        Title = "TP To Prehistoric Island",

    ToggleTPVolcano:OnChanged(function(Value)
        _G.TweenToPrehistoric = Value
    end)
    spawn(function()
        local island = game:GetService("Workspace").Map:WaitForChild("PrehistoricIsland")
    
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.TweenToPrehistoric then
                local prehistoricIslandCore = game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")
                if prehistoricIslandCore then
                    local relic = prehistoricIslandCore:FindFirstChild("Core") and prehistoricIslandCore.Core:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricRelic")
                    local skull = relic and relic:FindFirstChild("Skull")
                    if skull then
                        Tween(CFrame.new(skull.Position))
                        _G.TweenToPrehistoric = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    local TogglePatchVolcano = Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("TogglePatchVolcano", {
        Title = "Patch Volcano",
        Description = "",
        Default = false
    })
    
    TogglePatchVolcano:OnChanged(function(Value)
        _G.AutoVolcanic = Value
    end)
    
    spawn(function()
        while wait() do
            if _G.AutoVolcanic then
                local prompt = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") and workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Core") and workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild("ActivationPrompt") and workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.ActivationPrompt:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt")
    
                local promptSuccess = false
    
                if prompt and prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                    Tween(CFrame.new(prompt.Parent.Position))
                    fireproximityprompt(prompt, prompt.HoldDuration)
                    promptSuccess = true
                end
    
                pcall(function()
                    AutoHaki()
                end)
    
                pcall(function()
                    local lavaModel = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild("InteriorLava")
                    if lavaModel then
                        lavaModel:Destroy()
                    end
    
                    local island = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")
                    if island then
                        for _, obj in pairs(island:GetDescendants()) do
                            if (obj:IsA("Part") or obj:IsA("MeshPart")) and obj.Name:lower():find("lava") then
                                obj:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
    
                local rock = nil
                local rocksFolder = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild("VolcanoRocks")
                if rocksFolder then
                    for _, r in pairs(rocksFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        for _, n in {
                            "volcanorock",
                            "VFXLayer"
                        } do
                            local p = r:FindFirstChild(n)
                            if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
                                if p.Color == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 56) or p.Color == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 57) then
                                    rock = p
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if rock then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
    
                if rock then
                    Tween(CFrame.new(rock.Position))
                    local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local hrp = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp and (hrp.Position - rock.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
                        useAllSkill()
                    end
                elseif not promptSuccess then
                    local island = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")
                    if island then
                        local relic = island:FindFirstChild("Core") and island.Core:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricRelic")
                        local skull = relic and relic:FindFirstChild("Skull")
                        if skull then
                            Tween(CFrame.new(skull.Position))
                        end
                    end
                end
    
                pcall(function()
                    for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if enemy:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and enemy:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                sethiddenproperty(game.Players.LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
                                enemy.Humanoid.Health = 0
                                enemy.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                            until not _G.AutoVolcanic or not enemy.Parent or enemy.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("AttackLeviathanToggle", {
        Title = "Auto Leviathan",
        Description = "",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            _G.AttackLeviathan = v
    
            spawn(function()
                if _G.AttackLeviathan then
                    pcall(function()
                        game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("OpenLeviathanGate")
                    end)
                end
    
                local currentIndex = 1
    
                while task.wait() do
                    if _G.AttackLeviathan then
                        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                        local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        
                        if hrp then
                            local seaBeasts = workspace:FindFirstChild("SeaBeasts")
                            local segments = {}
    
                            if seaBeasts then
                                for _, obj in pairs(seaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
                                    if obj.Name == "Leviathan Segment" and obj:IsA("Model") and obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                        table.insert(segments, obj.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
    
                            if # segments > 0 then
                                if currentIndex > # segments then
                                    currentIndex = 1
                                end
    
                                local segment = segments[currentIndex]
                                if segment and segment:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                                    Tween(CFrame.new(segment.Position + Vector3.new(0, 500, 0)))
    
                                    local start = tick()
                                    while tick() - start < 30 and _G.AttackLeviathan do
                                        local dist = (hrp.Position - segment.Position).Magnitude
                                        if dist <= 5 then
                                            useAllSkill()
                                        end
                                        task.wait()
                                    end
                                end
    
                                currentIndex = currentIndex + 1
                            else
                                local tail = seaBeasts and seaBeasts:FindFirstChild("Leviathan Tail")
                                if tail and tail:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                    Tween(CFrame.new(tail.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 500, 0)))
                                    task.wait(1)
                                    local dist = (hrp.Position - tail.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                    if dist <= 5 then
                                        useAllSkill()
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    })
    local CollectValues = {}

    if Sea3 then
        table.insert(CollectValues, "Dino Bones")
        table.insert(CollectValues, "Eggs")
    end
    
    local selectedCollectType = nil     
    
    local DropdownCollectType = Tabs.Sea:AddDropdown("DropdownCollectType", {
        Title = "Select Collect",
        Values = CollectValues,
        Multi = false,
        Default = false
    })
    
    local ToggleAutoCollect = Tabs.Sea:AddToggle("ToggleAutoCollect", {
        Title = "Auto Collect",
        Description = "",
        Default = false
    })
    
    DropdownCollectType:SetValue("")
    
    DropdownCollectType:OnChanged(function(Value)
        selectedCollectType = Value
        if ToggleAutoCollect.Value then
            _G.AutoCollectBone = Value == "Dino Bones"
            _G.AutoCollectEgg = Value == "Eggs"
        else
            _G.AutoCollectBone = false
            _G.AutoCollectEgg = false
        end
    end)
    
    ToggleAutoCollect:OnChanged(function(Value)
        if not Value then
            _G.AutoCollectBone = false
            _G.AutoCollectEgg = false
        else
            _G.AutoCollectBone = selectedCollectType == "Dino Bones"
            _G.AutoCollectEgg = selectedCollectType == "Eggs"
        end
    end)
    
    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoCollectBone then
                for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name == "DinoBone" then
                        Tween(CFrame.new(obj.Position))
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                        until not obj or not obj.Parent
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    
    spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoCollectEgg then
                local dragonEggs = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.SpawnedDragonEggs:GetChildren()
                if # dragonEggs > 0 then
                    local randomEgg = dragonEggs[math.random(1, # dragonEggs)]
                    if randomEgg:IsA("Model") and randomEgg.PrimaryPart then
                        Tween(randomEgg.PrimaryPart.CFrame)
    
                        local prompt
                        for _, descendant in ipairs(randomEgg:GetDescendants()) do
                            if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and descendant.ActionText == "Collect" and descendant.ObjectText == "Dragon Egg" then
                                prompt = descendant
                                break
                            end
                        end
    
                        if prompt then
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                            until (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - randomEgg.PrimaryPart.Position).Magnitude <= 5)
    
                            fireproximityprompt(prompt, prompt.HoldDuration)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
function checkEagleEye()
    local islandModel = game:GetService("Workspace").Map.TikiOutpost.IslandModel
    local islandChunks = islandModel.IslandChunks and islandModel.IslandChunks.E
    local targetEyes = {
        Eye1 = false,
        Eye2 = false,
        Eye3 = false,
        Eye4 = false
    }

    for _, v in ipairs(islandModel:GetChildren()) do
        if string.match(v.Name, "^Eye%d$") and targetEyes[v.Name] ~= nil then
            if tonumber(v.Transparency) == 0 then
                targetEyes[v.Name] = true
            end
        end
    end

    if islandChunks then
        for _, v in ipairs(islandChunks:GetChildren()) do
            if string.match(v.Name, "^Eye%d$") and targetEyes[v.Name] ~= nil then
                if tonumber(v.Transparency) == 0 then
                    targetEyes[v.Name] = true
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for _, found in pairs(targetEyes) do
        if not found then
            return false
        end
    end

    return true
end

function DestroyTyrantSkies()
    local islandModel = game:GetService("Workspace").Map.TikiOutpost.IslandModel
    local islandChunks = islandModel and islandModel.IslandChunks and islandModel.IslandChunks.D
    local playerPosition = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position 

    if islandChunks and islandChunks:FindFirstChild("EagleBossArena") then
        for _, v in pairs(islandChunks.EagleBossArena:GetChildren()) do
            if v.Name == "Tree" then
                local treePosition = v.WorldPivot.Position
                local distance = (playerPosition - treePosition).Magnitude
                Tween(CFrame.new(treePosition))
                if distance <= 5 then
                    useAllSkill()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.AutoTyrant then
            pcall(function()
                local enemies = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies
                local tyrant = enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies")
                if tyrant then
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        Equip(SelectWeapon)
                        Tween(tyrant.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                        Attack()
                        AutoHaki()
                    until not tyrant.Parent or not enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies") or not _G.AutoTyrant
                elseif not checkEagleEye() then
                    local foundEnemy = false
                    for _, v in pairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if table.find({
                            "Serpent Hunter",
                            "Skull Slayer",
                            "Isle Champion",
                            "Sun-kissed Warrior"
                        }, v.Name) and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                            foundEnemy = true
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                Equip(SelectWeapon)
                                Tween(v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Pos)
                                Attack()
                                AutoHaki()
                                FarmPos = v.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                MonFarm = v.Name
                            until not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or checkEagleEye() or not _G.AutoTyrant
                        end
                    end
                    if not foundEnemy then
                        Tween(CFrame.new(-16665.48,105.23,1576.86))
                    end
                else
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                        DestroyTyrantSkies()
                    until enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies") or not _G.AutoTyrant
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

            end

end)

-- Fishing Modules
local Modules = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules")
local Net = Modules:WaitForChild("Net")
local FishReplicated = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("FishReplicated")
local FishingRequest = FishReplicated:FindFirstChild("FishingRequest")
local FishingClient = FishReplicated:WaitForChild("FishingClient")

local Util = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Util")
local GetWaterHeightAtLocation = require(Util:WaitForChild("GetWaterHeightAtLocation"))
local COMMF_ = Net:FindFirstChild("RF/JobsRemoteFunction")

-- Bait System
local Bailt = {
    ["Basic Bait"] = "Basic Bait",
    ["Kelp Bait"] = "Kelp Bait",
    ["Good Bait"] = "Good Bait",
    ["Abyssal Bait"] = "Abyssal Bait", 
    ["Frozen Bait"] = "Frozen Bait",

    ["Carnivore Bait"] = "Carnivore Bait",
}

for itemName, _ in pairs(Bailt) do
    table.insert(CraftBailtName, itemName)
end

    Multi = false,

    _G.SelectedBait = Value -- Update global bait selection
end)

-- Fishing Core Functions
local function EnsureRodAndBait()
    if not COMMF_ then return end
    pcall(function()
        COMMF_:InvokeServer("FishingNPC", "FirstTimeFreeRod")
        if _G.SelectedRod then
            COMMF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", _G.SelectedRod, {"Gear"})
        end
    end)

    task.wait(0.5)
    local FishingData = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Data") and LocalPlayer.Data:FindFirstChild("FishingData")
    if FishingData and (FishingData:GetAttribute("SelectedBait") == "None" or FishingData:GetAttribute("SelectedBait") == nil) then
        local success, inventory = pcall(function()
            return COMMF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
        end)
        if success and inventory then
            for _, v in pairs(inventory) do
                if v.Type == "Bait" and v.Name == _G.SelectedBait then
                    COMMF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", v.Name, {"Usables"})
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        if CraftRF and _G.SelectedBait then
            CraftRF:InvokeServer("Craft", _G.SelectedBait)
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end
end

local function EquipRod()
    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not Character then return end
    local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not Character:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod) then
        local BackpackRod = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)
        if BackpackRod and Humanoid then
            Humanoid:EquipTool(BackpackRod)
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end
end

local function GetCastPosition()
    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not Character then return end
    local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    local waterY = GetWaterHeightAtLocation(HumanoidRootPart.Position)
    local direction = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * (Config.Rod.MaxLaunchDistance or 50)

    local _, hitPos = Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(
        Ray.new(Character.Head.Position, direction),
        {Character, Workspace.Characters, Workspace.Enemies})

    if not hitPos then
        return HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)
    end

    return hitPos
end

local function CastLine()
    if not FishingRequest then return end
    pcall(function()
        FishingRequest:InvokeServer("StartCasting")
        task.wait(0.7)
        FishingRequest:InvokeServer("CastLineAtLocation", GetCastPosition(), 100, true)
    end)
end

local function CatchFish()
    if not FishingRequest then return end
    pcall(function()
        FishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catching", 1)
        task.wait(0.25)
        FishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catch", 1)
    end)
end

-- Water Detection (giữ nguyên từ code cũ)
local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
local WATER_TAG = "WaterBody"

local function detectWaterAround(pos)
    local nearestDist = math.huge
    local found = false
    for i = 0, 315, 45 do
        local dir = Vector3.new(math.cos(math.rad(i)), 0, math.sin(math.rad(i)))
        local rayOrigin = pos + Vector3.new(0, 15, 0)
        local rayDir = dir * 80 - Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
            workspace.Characters,
            workspace.Enemies
        }
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
        local result = workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, rayDir, params)
        if result and result.Instance then
            local name = result.Instance.Name:lower()
            if CollectionService:HasTag(result.Instance, WATER_TAG) or name:find("water") or name:find("plane") or name:find("base") then
                local distXZ = (Vector3.new(pos.X, 0, pos.Z) - Vector3.new(result.Position.X, 0, result.Position.Z)).Magnitude
                if distXZ < nearestDist then
                    nearestDist = distXZ
                    found = true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return found, nearestDist
end

local function IsNearWater(char)
    if not char then
        return false
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        return false
    end
    local pos = hrp.Position
    local groundRay = workspace:Raycast(pos, Vector3.new(0, -200, 0))
    local groundY = groundRay and groundRay.Position.Y or pos.Y
    local waterY = nil
    if GetWaterHeightAtLocation then
        pcall(function()
            waterY = GetWaterHeightAtLocation(pos)
        end)
    end
    local heightDiff = waterY and math.abs(groundY - waterY) or 0
    local near, dist = detectWaterAround(pos)
    if not near then
        return false
    end
    if heightDiff == 0 then
        return true
    end
    local allowedDiff = math.max(10, dist * 0.8)
    return heightDiff <= allowedDiff
end

-- Main Auto Fishing (sử dụng code từ file)
local AutoFishToggle = Tabs.Fish:AddToggle("AutoFish", {
    Title = "Auto Fish",
    Description = "",
    Default = false
})

AutoFishToggle:OnChanged(function(Value)
    _G.AutoFishing = Value
    if Value then
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.AutoFishing do
                task.wait(0.5)
                pcall(function()
                    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    if not Character then return end

                    -- Kiểm tra xem có ở gần nước không
                    if not IsNearWater(Character) then
                        warn("[AutoFishing] Not near water")
                        return
                    end

                    EnsureRodAndBait()
                    EquipRod()

                    local Rod = Character:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)
                    if Rod then
                        local state = Rod:GetAttribute("ServerState") or Rod:GetAttribute("State")
                        if state == "Biting" then
                            CatchFish()
                        elseif state == "ReeledIn" or state == "Idle" or not state then
                            CastLine()
                        end
                    else
                        warn("[AutoFishing] Rod not found in Character")
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

-- Position System (giữ nguyên)
local SavedPositions = {}

Tabs.Fish:AddButton({
    Title = "Save Position",
    Description = "",
    Callback = function()
        SavedPositions = {}
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            SavedPositions[1] = char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            Fluent:Notify({
                Title = "Save Complete",
                Duration = 10
            })
        end
    end
})

Tabs.Fish:AddButton({
    Title = "Delete Save Position",
    Description = "",
    Callback = function()
        SavedPositions = {}
        Fluent:Notify({
            Title = "Delete Complete",
            Duration = 10
        })
    end
})

-- Auto Fish với Position (kết hợp cả hai hệ thống)
local AutoFishPosToggle = Tabs.Fish:AddToggle("AutoFishPos", {
    Title = "Auto Fish (Position)",
    Description = "",
    Default = false
})

AutoFishPosToggle:OnChanged(function(Value)
    _G.AutoFishPos = Value
    if Value then
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.AutoFishPos do
                task.wait(0.5)
                pcall(function()
                    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    if not Character then return end

                    -- Di chuyển đến vị trí đã lưu
                    if SavedPositions and #SavedPositions > 0 then
                        Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = SavedPositions[#SavedPositions]
                        task.wait(1) -- Chờ di chuyển
                    end

                    EnsureRodAndBait()
                    EquipRod()

                    local Rod = Character:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)
                    if Rod then
                        local state = Rod:GetAttribute("ServerState") or Rod:GetAttribute("State")
                        if state == "Biting" then
                            CatchFish()
                        elseif state == "ReeledIn" or state == "Idle" or not state then
                            CastLine()
                        end
                    else
                        warn("[AutoFishPos] Rod not found in Character")
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
end)
